We study the practical goal of identifying the \topk{} items under a fixed comparison budget, and make two contributions. First, we cast judging as Bayesian inference over latent quality with explicit, judge-specific bias covariates (verbosity, position), regularized by a shrinkage prior so that the data decide which biases a given judge actually exhibits. Second, we introduce a \topk-aware active acquisition rule that chooses the next comparison to maximally reduce uncertainty about \topk{} \emph{membership}, rather than about the full ranking. On a controlled benchmark with known ground-truth quality, judged by sixteen real LLMs spanning open and proprietary families (Llama, Qwen, Phi-4, GPT-4o-mini/5.1/5.5, Gemini, DeepSeek, and Claude Haiku/Sonnet/Opus), naive aggregation plateaus at a wrong \topk{} on biased judges regardless of budget, while our bias-aware model recovers it; \topk-aware acquisition reaches this ceiling with far fewer comparisons than round-robin or a global-uncertainty (D-optimal) rule. Bias is real but heterogeneous and capability-dependent: cheap and mid-tier judges carry a strong verbosity bias that our model corrects (lifting recall from $\sim$$0.5$--$0.6$ to $0.84$--$1.0$), whereas the frontier judges we tested show little bias and already rank accurately, so bias-aware modeling changes little there.

</details>


### [151] [Predictive Conformal Slip Monitoring: An Empirical Evaluation of Rolling Split Conformal Prediction for Pre-Incident Traction Loss Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02124)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了基于滚动分裂共形预测（Rolling Split Conformal Prediction）的牵引力损失早期预警方法，发现该方法在19名车手、55,563条遥测样本中实现0.0精度和0.0召回率，同时产生15.3%的误报率，结论为负面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统牵引力控制架构仅在轮胎附着极限被突破后介入，本文希望探索一种基于统计的、可在牵引力完全丧失前发出预警的信号机制。

Method: 使用滚动分裂共形预测监控每位车手随机森林模型（预测期望滑移行为）的非一致性残差的波动性；与静态95分位数阈值基线对比；进行了残差自相关诊断（Ljung-Box检验）。

Result: 滚动波动率检测器在19名车手、55,563个测试样本中，针对14个真实事故标签的精确率和召回率均为0.0，平均标记15.3%的样本为异常；残差自相关检验显示所有车手均违反交换性假设（Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n=19/19）。

Conclusion: 该方法不能作为有效的早期预警信号；残差自相关违反了分裂共形预测的交换性假设，是导致高误报率的原因之一；需要更符合时间序列特性的预测方法。

Abstract: Conventional traction control architectures intervene only after the adhesion limit of a tire has already been breached. This paper investigates whether Rolling Split Conformal Prediction , monitoring the volatility of non-conformity residuals from a per-driver Random Forest model of expected slip behavior , can serve as a statistically grounded pre-incident warning signal, ahead of gross traction loss. Unlike an earlier internal draft of this work, the evaluation reported here corrects a confound in the slip proxy (vehicle speed is included as an explicit model feature, not left implicit in the target's denominator), uses every racing lap for each driver rather than only the fastest lap, and is scored against real, timestamped incident labels extracted from FIA Race Control Messages and track-limits lap deletions rather than narrated post-hoc. The result is negative: across 19 drivers and 55,563 test-phase telemetry samples, the rolling-volatility detector achieves a mean precision of essentially 0.0 and mean recall of 0.0 against 14 ground-truth incidents, while flagging on average 15.3% of all samples as anomalous , too high a false-alarm rate for any early-warning use. A static 95th-percentile threshold baseline performs no better in any way that would justify the added complexity of the conformal-volatility formulation. Residual autocorrelation diagnostics show the split-conformal exchangeability assumption is violated for every driver (Ljung-Box p < 0.001, n = 19/19), which is one plausible driver of the high false-alarm rate. We report this as a methodologically rigorous negative finding, diagnose its likely causes, and outline what a genuinely predictive version of this approach would require.

</details>


### [152] [ART for Diffusion Sampling: Continuous-Time Control and Actor-Critic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02137)
*Yilie Huang,Wenpin Tang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ART(自适应重参数化时间)及ART-RL方法，通过学习扩散采样中的时间步分配，将时间变形率作为控制变量，利用连续时间强化学习框架优化时间调度，在保持采样管线不变的情况下显著提升样本质量，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型采样通常采用均匀或手工设计的时间步调度，这些固定策略可能是次优的，缺乏自适应性，无法根据采样轨迹动态调整时间步分配。

Method: 将时间步分配问题建模为连续时间控制问题(ART)，通过重参数化学习时钟的速度(时间变形率)；进一步引入随机化辅助公式(ART-RL)，将其转化为连续时间强化学习问题，采用高斯策略，推导出策略评估与改进的轨迹矩恒等式，实现演员-评论家更新。

Result: 在低维可控实验到图像生成任务中，ART-RL仅通过改变时间步网格即可改进现有扩散采样器，在相同计算预算下持续优于强基线调度；学习到的调度具有良好的泛化性，可跨采样预算、数据集、求解器、流程和表示空间迁移而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: ART-RL提供了一种原则性的、可即插即用的时间步优化方法，证明了通过强化学习学习扩散采样调度的有效性，且最优高斯策略等价于原始ART问题的最优解。

Abstract: We study timestep allocation for score-based diffusion sampling, where a learned reverse-time dynamics is discretized on a finite grid. Uniform and hand-crafted schedules are standard choices, but they rely on fixed prescriptions and can therefore be suboptimal. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Reparameterized Time (ART), a continuous-time control formulation that learns a time change by treating the speed of the sampling clock as the control, so that a uniform grid on the learned clock induces adaptive timesteps in the original diffusion time. Based on a leading-order Euler error surrogate, ART provides a principled objective for allocating timesteps along the sampling trajectory. To solve this deterministic control problem, we introduce ART-RL, an auxiliary randomized formulation with Gaussian policies that turns schedule learning into a continuous-time reinforcement learning problem. We prove that the randomized ART-RL formulation is equivalent to ART at the optimizer level, in the sense that its optimal Gaussian policy recovers the optimal ART time-warping rate through its mean. We further establish policy evaluation and policy improvement characterizations and derive trajectory-based moment identities that yield implementable actor--critic updates for learning the schedule. Across experiments ranging from controlled low-dimensional settings to image generation, ART-RL can be plugged into existing diffusion samplers by changing only the timestep grid, consistently improving sample quality over strong baseline schedules at matched budgets while leaving the rest of the sampling pipeline unchanged. The learned schedules also exhibit broad generalization, transferring without retraining across sampling budgets, datasets, solvers, pipelines, and representation spaces.

</details>


### [153] [Probing Chemical Language Models: Effects of Pre-training and Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02140)
*Anna Karnysheva,Dietrich Klakow,Ji-Ung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了化学语言模型（CLMs）对78种分子子结构的编码能力，发现预训练能提升模型对分子结构的感知（尤其上层），随机初始化模型在第一层就能编码环结构，而微调会增强任务相关子结构的表征变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学语言模型使用SMILES等线性化表示进行训练，但学界尚不清楚它们究竟编码了哪些化学上有意义的子结构，因此有必要系统探究CLMs对分子子结构的内在学习表征。

Method: 对8个预训练模型和6个随机初始化模型，探测78种分子子结构的表征编码情况；进一步分析在两种化学下游任务上进行微调后，模型对分子子结构的表征如何变化。

Result: 预训练普遍提升了CLMs对分子结构的感知，尤其是上层表征；随机初始化模型在第一层已能较好地编码环结构；微调对任务相关的分子子结构影响更大，且这种变化符合化学理论。

Conclusion: 预训练增强了CLMs对分子结构的理解能力，微调则进一步有针对性地强化了任务相关子结构的表征，且模型的行为与化学知识一致，揭示了CLMs内部表征的可解释性。

Abstract: Chemical language models (CLMs) are trained with linearized representations such as SMILES, yet it remains unclear which chemically meaningful substructures they encode. To foster a better understanding of CLMs, we conduct a systematic study and probe for 78 molecular substructures across eight pre-trained and six randomly initialized models. We furthermore study how fine-tuning on chemical downstream tasks affects the learned representations of molecular substructures. Our results show that pre-training generally improves molecular structure awareness of CLMs, particularly in the upper layers. Moreover, randomly initialized models already encode ring structures well in the first layer. Our analysis on two chemical downstream tasks further reveals that, interestingly, fine-tuning affects task-relevant molecular substructures more than others, indicating that the changes in the representations follow chemical theory.

</details>


### [154] [Predicting Early Stages Of Alzheimer's Disease And Identifying Key Biomarkers Using Deep Artificial Neural Network And Ensemble Of Machine Learning Methodologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02142)
*Debopriya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的阿尔茨海默病早期检测模型，采用迭代插补处理缺失值、Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类别不平衡、包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择，构建了集成学习模型（逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN、LightGBM）和人工神经网络，在ADNI数据集上取得了良好性能，并识别出有助于早期诊断的重要生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期症状轻微易被误认为正常衰老，导致多数患者确诊时病情已进展，错失最佳干预时机。目前尚无完全治愈方法，早期检测对于改善疾病管理和延缓进展至关重要。

Method: 使用ADNI数据集，首先采用迭代插补处理缺失值，然后使用Borderline SVM-SMOTE处理类别不平衡问题，接着通过包装法和嵌入法进行特征选择。构建了由逻辑回归、Extra Trees、Bagging KNN和LightGBM作为基分类器的堆叠集成模型，同时训练了人工神经网络。模型性能通过精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC进行评估。

Result: 研究比较了集成模型与人工神经网络的性能，通过精确率、召回率、F1分数和AUC-ROC等指标进行评估，并识别出可用于早期诊断的重要生物标志物。

Conclusion: 提出的机器学习方法能够有效检测阿尔茨海默病早期阶段，其中堆叠集成模型和人工神经网络均表现良好，研究识别出的生物标志物对临床早期诊断具有参考价值。

Abstract: Alzheimers disease (AD) is a brain disorder that develops slowly and mainly affects memory, thinking, language, and daily activities. It is one of the most common causes of dementia and creates many difficulties for patients as well as their families. In the early stage, the symptoms are often mild and may look like normal ageing. For this reason, many people are diagnosed late, when the disease has already progressed. At present, there is no complete cure for AD. Still, early detection can help doctors manage the condition better and take suitable steps at the right time. In this study, a machine learning model is proposed to detect the early stages of Alzheimers disease using clinical details, neuropsychological test scores, and neuroimaging-related measures. The data used in this work is collected from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI). As the dataset has missing values, iterative imputation is applied to fill them. The dataset also has class imbalance, which is handled using Borderline SVM-SMOTE. After that, feature selection is carried out using wrapper-based and embedded methods so that only important features are used for training. The selected features are divided into training and testing sets, and feature scaling is applied. A stacking ensemble model is developed using Logistic Regression, Extra Trees, Bagging KNN, and LightGBM as base classifiers. Along with this, an artificial neural network is also trained on the same dataset. The performance of these models is compared using precision, recall, F1-score, and AUC-ROC. This study aims to find the best classifier and also identify important biomarkers that may help in the early diagnosis of Alzheimers disease.

</details>


### [155] [Dynamic Neural Graph Encoding of Inference Processes in Deep Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02166)
*Di Wu,Huan Liu,Zhixiang Chi,Yuanhao Yu,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态神经图编码器(DNG-Encoder)，通过将神经网络参数建模为动态图来捕捉推理过程中的时序动态性，并在隐式神经表示分类等下游任务上超越现有方法约10%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法处理高维权重空间时效率低下，且忽略了神经网络推理中层与层之间顺序处理的时序特性。

Method: 提出DNG-Encoder，使用动态图表示神经网络参数以捕获推理的时序动态；并基于此开发INR2JLS框架将隐式神经表示映射到联合隐空间，用于下游分类任务。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-INR数据集上，INR分类准确率超越现有最优方法约10%。

Conclusion: 动态图建模权重空间可以有效保留神经网络的时序处理特性，显著提升对隐式神经表示的分析与分类能力。

Abstract: The rapid advancements in using neural networks as implicit data representations have attracted significant interest in developing machine learning methods that analyze and process the weight spaces of other neural networks. However, efficiently handling these highdimensional weight spaces remains challenging. Existing methods often overlook the sequential nature of layer-by-layer processing in neural network inference. In this work, we propose a novel approach using dynamic graphs to represent neural network parameters, capturing the temporal dynamics of inference. Our Dynamic Neural Graph Encoder (DNG-Encoder) processes these graphs, preserving the sequential nature of neural processing. Additionally, we also leverage DNG-Encoder to develop INR2JLS (Implicit Neural Representation to Joint Latent Space) for facilitate downstream applications, such as classifying Implicit Neural Representations (INRs). Our approach demonstrates significant improvements across multiple tasks, surpassing the state-of-the-art INR classification accuracy by approximately 10% on the CIFAR-100-INR.

</details>


### [156] [Privacy-Preserving and Verifiable Approximate Distributed Coded Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02187)
*Xavier Martínez-Luaña,Alba Gude-Santos,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模型无关的对抗鲁棒分布式学习框架，通过结合GPBACC隐私增强编码计算技术与范式特定的防御机制（联邦学习中的鲁棒聚合、去中心化学习中的近似解码比较和群组测试），在统一框架下同时解决隐私保护和恶意行为防御问题，实验证明该方法能有效降低隐私泄露并提升对主动攻击者的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法通常孤立地处理隐私泄露和恶意操纵威胁，且往往针对特定学习范式或模型架构，缺乏统一的适用框架；联邦学习和去中心化学习具有不同的对抗面，但很少被统一处理。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的统一框架：(1) 结合GPBACC（一种适用于任意机器学习模型的隐私增强编码计算技术）；(2) 联邦学习中集成鲁棒聚合策略缓解恶意参与者影响；(3) 去中心化学习中采用近似解码比较和群组测试技术，无需可信聚合器即可实现轻量级验证和对手隔离。

Result: 通过明确的攻击驱动分析，实现代表性隐私攻击和恶意行为，实验表明GPBACC与鲁棒聚合和验证机制的结合能显著降低隐私泄露并提升对主动攻击者的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 隐私增强编码计算与适当的对抗鲁棒策略相结合，为安全分布式机器学习提供了实用且可部署的基础。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning enables collaborative model training without centralizing data, but it also exposes learning processes to privacy leakage and malicious manipulation. Existing defenses typically address these threats in isolation and are often tailored to specific learning paradigms or model architectures, limiting their applicability in realistic deployments. In particular, federated learning and decentralized learning exhibit distinct adversarial surfaces that are rarely addressed within a unified framework. In this paper, we present a model-agnostic framework for adversary-resistant distributed learning that jointly addresses privacy preservation and malicious behavior across both federated and decentralized settings. Our approach combines paradigm-specific defense mechanisms with GPBACC, a privacy-enhancing coded computing technique applicable to arbitrary machine learning models. For federated learning, we integrate robust aggregation strategies to mitigate the impact of malicious participants, while for decentralized learning we employ approximate decode-and-compare and group testing techniques to enable lightweight verification and adversary isolation without relying on a trusted aggregator. Crucially, we evaluate the proposed framework through an explicit, attack-driven analysis. We implement representative privacy attacks and malicious behaviors, and empirically demonstrate that the combination of GPBACC with robust aggregation and verification mechanisms significantly reduces privacy leakage and improves resilience against active adversaries. These results suggest that privacy-enhancing coded computing, when combined with appropriate adversary-resistance strategies, provides a practical and deployable foundation for secure distributed machine learning.

</details>


### [157] [An Optimisation Framework for the Well-Conditioned Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02194)
*Joseph Webb,Sadok Jerad,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DSGNAR（双重素描高斯-牛顿自适应比率优化框架），通过二阶优化解决PINN的严重病态损失景观问题，在多种PDE问题上实现了前所未有的精度（相对ℓ2误差低至3×10⁻¹⁶）和速度（单精度下<10秒达到4.75×10⁻⁷误差），大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在求解偏微分方程时因损失景观严重病态而难以达到传统求解器的精度，其根本障碍在于优化困难，现有方法无法有效解决这一病态问题。

Method: 提出DSGNAR框架：双重素描高斯-牛顿模型（双随机采样技术降低计算复杂度）结合自适应正则化与步长控制策略，通过精心调控正则化和步长来应对损失景观的病态性。

Result: 在非线性、混沌、多尺度、高维和Navier-Stokes等多类问题中：双精度下相对ℓ2误差低至3×10⁻¹⁶；在经典Burgers方程上比当代结果提升五个数量级；在高维Poisson问题上提升八个数量级；单精度下Burgers方程ℓ2^rel=4.75×10⁻⁷，耗时不到10秒；对网络架构、算术精度和初始超参数选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSGNAR通过二阶优化有效解决了PINN的病态优化问题，在精度和速度上均取得显著突破，为PINN达到传统求解器精度水平提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising route to solve partial differential equations, yet they have struggled to reach the precision of classical solvers. The obstacle is increasingly understood to be one of optimisation, owing to the severely ill-conditioned loss landscape. We present $\textbf{DSGNAR}$: Doubly-Sketched Gauss-Newton with Adaptive Ratio, a scalable second-order optimisation framework that confronts this ill-conditioning and, in doing so, obtains unprecedented accuracy and speed. $\textbf{DSGNAR}$ couples a doubly-sketched Gauss-Newton model with a novel strategy that carefully controls both regularisation and step length. Across a suite of problems spanning nonlinear, chaotic, multi-scale, high-dimensional, and Navier-Stokes, the framework greatly improves on the state of the art: able to attain relative $\ell_2$ errors as low as $3\times10^{-16}$ in double precision, improve contemporary results by five orders of magnitude on the canonical Burgers' equation, and as much as eight orders on a high-dimensional Poisson problem, while remaining markedly faster. We further show that, in single precision, solutions at the limit of round-off error can be obtained very quickly: Burgers' equation to $\ell_2^{\text{rel}} = 4.75 \times 10^{-7}$ in under ten seconds. The framework is also robust to the choice of architecture, arithmetic precision, and initial hyperparameters.
  The code is available at https://www.github.com/wephy/physics-informed-neural-networks

</details>


### [158] [Online Resource Allocation with Continuous Random Consumption: Regret under Degeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02196)
*Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线资源分配问题，其中奖励和资源消耗量均可连续分布。请求按可观察类型到达，需在固定容量下不可逆地接受或拒绝。通过定义活跃加权质量指数p，揭示了遗憾上界与下界：当p>1时，任何在线策略至少产生Ω(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})遗憾，且样本路径边际策略可达该下界（忽略多对数因子）；当p=1时，该策略实现O((log T)^2)遗憾。该策略无需流体非退化假设，即可在整个正则性类中实现o(√T)遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线资源分配研究多假设奖励或消耗量为离散分布或满足特定正则条件，缺乏对连续分布场景下（特别是流体松弛退化时）遗憾界的一般性刻画。

Method: 提出活跃加权质量指数p来量化价值-大小比率接近截止阈值的请求质量；基于此建立遗憾下界（对任意p>1），并设计样本路径边际策略匹配下界（当p>1时）或实现对数平方遗憾（当p=1时）。

Result: 当p>1时，下界为Ω(T^{1/2-1/(2p)})，上界匹配至多对数因子；当p=1时，上界为O((log T)^2)。独立均匀分布示例中：大小与价值-大小比独立时p=1；大小与奖励独立时p=2。

Conclusion: 无需流体非退化假设（允许原始退化和对偶非唯一性），该策略即可在整个正则性类中实现o(√T)遗憾。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation when both rewards and consumption sizes may be continuously distributed. Requests arrive sequentially and must be accepted or rejected irrevocably under fixed resource capacities. Each request belongs to one of finitely many observable types; conditional on an observable request type, both the reward and the scalar size are random, and the realized size scales a fixed type-specific resource-consumption vector. The model allows the deterministic fluid relaxation to be degenerate.
  We show that additive regret is governed by the size-weighted mass of requests whose value-to-size ratios lie near the active acceptance cutoffs. We formalize this quantity through an active weighted-mass exponent p. When p > 1, this cutoff mass is thin, and the problem is genuinely hard: every online policy must incur regret of order at least $T^{1/2 - 1/(2p)}$, and this holds for every p > 1. A sample-path marginal policy matches this lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors; and when p = 1, so that the mass grows linearly near the cutoff, it attains $O((\log T)^2)$ regret. For example, if the size and the value-to-size ratio are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 1; if instead the size and the reward are independent and uniformly distributed, then p = 2. Thus the policy achieves $o(\sqrt{T})$ regret throughout this regularity class without any fluid non-degeneracy assumption, allowing both primal degeneracy and dual non-uniqueness.

</details>


### [159] [Self-explainable Operator Learning for Discovering Spatial Patterns in Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02203)
*Mojgan Alishiri,Amirhossein Arzani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自解释的算子学习框架，通过将算子学习重构为积分方程线性组合，利用积分可加分解性将输入域划分为子域并计算局部积分贡献，从而实现对函数到标量和函数到函数映射的输入-输出区域级解释。在血流和非定常气动等流体问题中验证表明，模型优先关注高梯度特征区域，与事后解释方法定性一致，且可解释性内嵌于算子结构本身，无需外部工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的算子学习模型是不透明的黑箱，难以解释其预测背后的推理过程，限制了在科学应用中的可信度。

Method: 将算子学习重构为广义函数线性模型的线性组合（通过积分方程表达），利用积分方程的加性可分解性，将输入域划分为子域并计算局部积分，以评估各区域对最终预测的贡献，从而实现直接可解释性。

Result: 在血流动力学和非定常气动弹性问题中，算子优先关注具有强特征梯度的区域，提供物理上有意义的洞察；与事后解释方法定性一致，且可解释性内嵌于算子结构内部。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种数学透明且物理可解释的方法来揭示数据关系，无需外部解释工具，增强了机器学习在科学应用中的可信度，促进对物理系统进行更明智的数据驱动分析。

Abstract: Operator learning has emerged as a powerful tool for modeling complex physical systems in functional spaces. However, their neural network-based architectures make them opaque models, obscuring the reasoning behind their predictions. In this work, we introduce a self-explainable operator learning framework that overcomes this challenge by reformulating operator learning as a linear combination of generalized functional linear models expressed through integral equations. Exploiting the additive decomposability of these integral equations, we divide the input domain into subdomains and compute localized integrals to evaluate the contribution of each region to the final prediction. This decomposition enables direct interpretability where the model explains both inputs and outputs by linking specific input regions to corresponding output patterns, thereby revealing which spatial features drive predictions. We demonstrate the framework on function-to-scalar and function-to-function mappings in fluid flow problems involving blood flow and unsteady aerodynamics. The results show that the operator most often prioritizes regions with strong feature gradients, providing physically meaningful insight into the model's decision-making process. Comparisons with established post-hoc explainability methods demonstrate qualitative agreement while highlighting the key advantage of the proposed approach: explainability is embedded directly within the operator structure itself and does not require an external tool. Therefore, our framework provides a mathematically transparent and physically interpretable approach to uncover relationships within data, fostering trust in machine learning for scientific applications by enabling more informed data-driven analysis of physical systems.

</details>


### [160] [Generalization in offline RL: The structure is more important than the amount of pessimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02288)
*Max Weltevrede,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Wendelin Böhmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在离线强化学习的上下文MDP中，过度悲观并不会阻碍最优泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否尊重最优解的对称性；并据此提出在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失进行数据增强，优于常规的增强数据集训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究认为离线RL中过度悲观会阻碍泛化，本文试图澄清悲观程度与泛化能力之间的关系，指出真正影响泛化的是悲观结构是否与最优解的对称性一致，而非悲观程度本身。

Method: 理论证明：非对称的轻度悲观值函数可能比对称的过度悲观值函数泛化更差。实践方法：受理论启发，提出在策略提取阶段通过一致性损失应用数据增强，而非在常规离线训练中使用增强数据集。实验验证：在旋转对称reacher环境中使用IQL和CQL进行实证验证。

Result: 理论上证明了悲观结构是否对称比悲观程度对泛化影响更大；实验上验证了在策略提取阶段使用一致性损失的DA方法优于传统增强数据集训练方法。

Conclusion: 离线RL中过度悲观本身不会阻碍最优泛化，关键在于悲观结构是否匹配最优解的对称性；数据增强的最优应用方式是策略提取阶段的 consistency loss，而非离线训练阶段的增强数据集。

Abstract: While pessimism counteracts overestimation bias in offline reinforcement learning (RL), being overly conservative has been associated with hindering certain forms of generalization. However, in this paper we demonstrate that being overly pessimistic does not inherently prevent optimal generalization in contextual MDPs (CMDPs). Instead, we argue successful generalization depends not on the amount of pessimism, but whether the pessimistic structure respects the underlying symmetries of the optimal solution. We prove that a mildly pessimistic, non-symmetric value function can generalize worse than an overly pessimistic, symmetric one. In offline RL, the structure of the pessimism is determined by the structure of the dataset coverage. As such, enforcing a symmetric value function can be non-trivial, and might require techniques such as data augmentation (DA). Inspired by our theoretical results, we argue that DA can best be applied through a consistency loss during policy extraction, rather than the common practice of (regular) offline training on an augmented dataset. This is empirically validated using IQL and CQL on a rotationally symmetric reacher environment.

</details>


### [161] [Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02291)
*Ruihang Li,Mengde Xu,Shuyang Gu,Leigang Qu,Fuli Feng,Han Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分布奖励的强化学习框架，通过子集替换策略和后验模型合并系数优化，有效缓解了视觉生成模型中的奖励黑客问题和模式坍缩，显著提升了FID和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉生成强化学习采用样本级奖励函数，导致奖励黑客问题，降低图像多样性并产生视觉异常。

Method: 1) 采用分布奖励而非样本奖励，使生成样本与真实数据分布对齐；2) 提出子集替换策略降低分布奖励的计算成本；3) 使用RL优化后验模型合并系数，缓解SDE引入的训练-推理不一致。

Result: SiT模型FID-50K从8.30降至5.77，EDM2模型从3.74降至3.52；定性评估显示感知质量提升并保持样本多样性。

Conclusion: 分布奖励框架有效解决了样本级奖励导致的模式坍缩和奖励黑客问题，在保持多样性的同时提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Conventional reinforcement learning strategies for visual generation typically employ sample-wise reward functions, yet this practice frequently results in reward hacking that degrades image diversity and introduces visual anomalies. To address these limitations, we present a novel framework that finetunes generative models using distribution-wise rewards, ensuring better alignment with real-world data distributions. Unlike rewards that evaluate samples individually, distribution-wise reward accounts for the data distribution of the samples, mitigating the mode collapse problem that occurs when all samples optimize towards the same direction independently. To overcome the prohibitive computational cost of estimating these rewards, we introduce a subset-replace strategy that efficiently provides reward signals by updating only a small subset of a generated reference set. Additionally, we apply RL to optimize post-hoc model merging coefficients, potentially mitigating the train-inference inconsistency caused by introducing stochastic differential equation (SDE) in regular RL practices. Extensive experiments show our approach significantly improves FID-50K across various base models, from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and from 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2. Qualitative evaluation also confirms that our method enhances perceptual quality while preserving sample diversity.

</details>


### [162] [One More Time: Revisiting Neural Quantum States from a Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02292)
*Juan Agustín Duque,Sergio García Heredia,Vinicius Hernandes,Eliška Greplová,Thomas Spriggs,Aaron Courville,Anna Dawid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近端波函数优化（PWO），一种基于信赖域的自回归神经量子态训练算法，通过分别裁剪振幅概率比和相位增量来稳定变分能量最小化，在伊辛和阻挫自旋系统中优于Adam、minSR和SPRING，并成功微调了15亿参数的RWKV-7模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经量子态（NQS）中自回归模型的优化方法存在空白：Adam可扩展但忽略函数空间几何，随机重构（SR）理论上严格但计算昂贵且数值不稳定。需要一种兼具可扩展性和理论保证的优化算法。

Method: 将变分能量最小化重新表述为Born分布上的优势策略梯度问题，进而引入信赖域优化。提出PWO算法，分别对振幅通道的概率比变化和相位通道的相位增量进行裁剪，避免显式矩阵求逆，并支持样本复用。

Result: 在一维和二维伊辛及阻挫J1-J2自旋系统中，PWO在稳定性和收敛速度上优于Adam、minSR和SPRING。成功将NQS优化扩展到15亿参数的RWKV-7模型，规模比此前工作大三个数量级。

Conclusion: PWO结合了一阶优化的可扩展性和信赖域方法的理论保证，为大规模NQS训练提供了一种实用且稳定的方案，显著拓展了NQS的应用边界。

Abstract: Neural quantum states (NQS) provide a flexible and scalable framework for approximating quantum many-body wavefunctions. Among NQS parameterizations, autoregressive models are especially attractive because they enable exact, independent sampling from the Born distribution, avoiding the autocorrelation and mixing issues of Markov chain methods. Yet their optimization remains comparatively underexplored: Adam is a scalable method but ignores function space geometry, while stochastic reconfiguration is principled but costly and numerically fragile in large models. To address this gap, we show that variational energy minimization can be viewed as an advantage policy-gradient problem over the Born distribution, motivating trust-region optimization for NQS training. We introduce Proximal Wavefunction Optimization (PWO), a principled trust-region algorithm that clips probability-ratio changes in the amplitude channel and phase increments in the phase channel. PWO avoids explicit matrix inversion, reuses samples across multiple updates, and combines the scalability of first-order optimization with theoretical guarantees. Across Ising and frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ one- and two-dimensional spin systems, PWO improves stability and wall-clock convergence over Adam, minSR, and SPRING. Finally, we fine-tune a $1.5$B-parameter RWKV-7 model, demonstrating NQS optimization at a scale over three orders of magnitude beyond prior work.

</details>


### [163] [Self-Gating Attention for Efficient Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02344)
*Dezheng Wang,Tong Chen,Wei Yuan,Congyan Chen,Shihua Li,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自门控注意力（SGA），一种即插即用的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过共享可学习矩阵和输入相关残差分量替代标准自注意力中的QK计算，在保持预测性能的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力在时间序列预测中具有二次时间和内存复杂度，限制了其在资源受限或高吞吐量系统中的应用。作者观察到时间序列预测中自注意力图存在跨时间步的冗余模式，这为设计更高效的注意力机制提供了契机。

Method: 提出Self-Gating Attention (SGA)，用共享可学习矩阵捕获通用注意力模式，结合输入相关残差分量捕获输入特定的变化，从而避免查询和键的投影计算，实现与回看长度呈线性的时间和得分矩阵内存复杂度。

Result: 在涵盖电力、金融、天气、医疗监测、人体活动和气候记录的9个公开数据集上，将SGA集成到多个预测骨干网络中，与标准自注意力和轻量级注意力变体对比，SGA在保持竞争性预测性能的同时提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: SGA是一种可用于资源受限部署场景的高效时间序列预测注意力机制，提供了面向部署的实证证据。

Abstract: Transformer architectures have shown strong potential in time series forecasting, where multi-head self-attention is widely used to capture temporal dependencies across historical timestamps. However, standard self-attention has quadratic time and memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. This cost may limit its use in resource-constrained or high-throughput forecasting systems, where fast and memory-efficient inference is important. Through qualitative and quantitative analyses, we observe that self-attention maps in time series forecasting often contain redundant patterns across different timestamps. This phenomenon can be related to the repeated temporal patterns and relatively stable temporal correlations in many real-world time series. Motivated by this observation, we propose Self-Gating Attention (SGA), a plug-and-play attention mechanism that represents the attention score with a shared learnable matrix and an input-dependent residual component. The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [164] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为可独立求解的子模块并重组，将搜索空间从GPU采样转移到CPU重组，在代码生成任务上相比标准RL和多样性优化RL基线，GPU token成本降低约50倍，并解决了标准方法无法触及的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和强化学习）在基策略生成正确解概率接近零时均失效，因为搜索空间过大无法通过更多采样或梯度信号克服。作者试图通过任务分解来降低问题难度，而非增加采样强度。

Method: 提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习显式学习将问题分解为层级化代码结构并独立实现各子模块。重组k个模块的n个实现可产生k^n个候选解，将瓶颈从GPU推理转移到CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B, Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准和多样性优化RL基线；GPU token成本降低约50倍；能解决标准生成方法无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 通过分解任务而非强化采样，可以在基策略几乎无法生成正确解时仍有效解决问题，且大幅降低计算成本。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [165] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NeuFS，一种基于神经元激活模式的主动少样本学习框架，通过分析模型内部动态而非输出层信号来选择最有价值的标注样本，在推理和文本分类任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动少样本学习方法依赖输出层信号（如预测熵、基于外部嵌入的语义相似度）来选择样本，忽略了模型内部动态（如神经元激活模式）所蕴含的知识缺口信息。

Method: NeuFS 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用神经元激活模式直接表征样本；(2) 双准则选择策略：基于神经元模式的多样性选择确保样本覆盖广度，以及通过量化神经元共识来识别模型易产生幻觉的信息性/挑战性样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS 在推理和文本分类任务上均优于现有主动少样本学习方法。消融实验验证了内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供了更有效、更 principled 的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS 通过将样本选择从输出层代理信号转向模型内部神经元动力学，为主动少样本学习提供了更本质有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [166] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedAgent是一个混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，用于多智能体活动识别。它通过变分量子电路融合模块（仅需72个量子旋转参数，相比经典MLP融合的33K参数减少约10倍）对加速度计-陀螺仪交互进行量子态编码和纠缠建模，在OPPORTUNITY数据集上实现97.7%的平均测试准确率，证明参数高效的量子融合与传统联邦基线方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私敏感的机器人感知应用中具有潜力，但多智能体系统产生的异构非独立同分布（non-IID）多模态传感器流会降低传统联邦学习算法的性能，同时经典融合模块引入大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，集成变分量子电路融合模块，通过量子态编码和纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互。量子融合仅需72个量子旋转参数，而经典MLP融合需33K参数。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，基于subject-based non-IID划分的条件下，达到97.7%的平均测试准确率，参数总量减少约10倍。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合模块在保持与传统联邦基线方法竞争性能的同时，大幅减少了参数规模和通信开销。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [167] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Exformer模型，通过极端自适应注意力机制（Local、Stride、Extreme三个稀疏组件）显式建模时间序列中的正常事件与罕见极端事件之间的依赖关系，在四个真实水文径流数据集上实现了优于现有方法的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中的罕见极端事件（如洪峰）对预测至关重要，但现有Transformer模型均匀对待所有时间点，导致对极端模式的表征不足。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，包含极端自适应注意力机制，由三个稀疏注意力组件构成：Local（捕捉短期依赖）、Stride（捕捉周期性依赖）、Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端径流模式之间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于多个最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力能够提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [168] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，发现掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且分布式系统的鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而增强。基于理论发现，提出了带有局部到全局对齐正则化的MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论分析的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式自监督学习方法面临数据异构性的关键挑战，但缺乏对不同D-SSL框架在该挑战下鲁棒性的理论理解。

Method: 提出严格的数学理论分析，证明MIM预训练比CL对异构数据更鲁棒，分析网络连通性与鲁棒性的关系；并基于理论提出MAR损失（MIM目标+局部到全局对齐正则化）。

Result: 理论上证明MIM比CL对非IID数据更鲁棒，联邦学习（FL）的鲁棒性不低于去中心化学习（DecL）；实验验证了理论发现并证明MAR损失的有效性。

Conclusion: 为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论指导；MAR损失作为理论的实际应用，进一步验证了分析的正确性。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [169] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用神经元激活信号指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过同策略自蒸馏实现无需标注的LLM后训练，在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持跨领域泛化能力并缓解校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无标注自进化方法（如基于SFT/GRPO和基于奖励的RL变体）存在领域外性能下降或校准误差膨胀问题，且专家标注在专业领域成本高昂，因此需要一种无需真实世界交互反馈或人工标注监督的高效后训练方法。

Method: 提出Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD)，一种数据驱动的无标注自蒸馏框架。利用内部神经元激活信号同时指导（1）训练数据选择和（2）教师上下文构建，然后通过从教师分布进行同策略蒸馏来训练模型，整个过程无需任何真实标签。

Result: 在多个专业领域基准上，Neuron-OPSD在提升领域内任务性能的同时，保持了跨领域泛化能力，并且相比之前无标注基线方法缓解了校准误差膨胀问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD框架特别适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂不可行的场景，在概念上区别于依赖离线轨迹和奖励标注的离线RL方法，为无标注自进化LLM后训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [170] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构化优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与传统Adam在NequIP和Allegro MLIP模型训练中的表现，发现SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上显著优于Adam，尤其是在部分力监督场景下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP训练中优化器的选择被长期忽视，社区默认使用Adam及其变体，而对于新架构和数据集的研究已很多，优化器这一潜在的设计轴尚未被系统探索。

Method: 实现了Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon三种矩阵结构化优化器，并在NequIP和Allegro两种MLIP模型上进行系统对比实验，评估其在收敛速度和最终精度上的表现。

Result: SOAP和SOAP-Muon在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；Muon仅相对于Adam有部分提升；在部分力监督条件下，这些优化器的优势更为突出。

Conclusion: 优化器的选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，SOAP和SOAP-Muon是比Adam更优的选择。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [171] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD提出选择性教师指导机制，通过反向KL重心目标平衡教师-学生分布，有效缓解特权信息泄漏并保持探索能力，在科学推理任务上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中教师的密集token级监督存在两个关键问题：1）特权信息泄漏——学生编码了测试时不可用的答案相关捷径；2）过拟合导致探索抑制和跨域泛化下降。现有方法无法从根本上解决这些问题。

Method: DemoPSD通过选择性采用教师指导，构建教师和学生分布的几何加权组合——反向KL重心目标（reverse-KL barycenter target），并利用分布差异自适应控制每个token位置的混合比例，从而在向教师学习与保持学生自身推理能力之间取得自然平衡。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO；在OOD的GPQA基准上表现鲁棒泛化；维持更高的训练熵（即更好的探索能力）。理论证明实现了泄漏衰减和探索保持。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过选择性教师指导策略，从根本上解决了OPSD中的特权信息泄漏和探索抑制问题，为LLM推理训练提供了更鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Field-Deployable RF Capture System for Indoor, Outdoor, and Foliage Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01368)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Peter B. Foytik,Jared Cochran,Jeff Bobrow,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HackRF One、Raspberry Pi 5和GNSS的紧凑型、电池供电的便携式射频捕获系统，可连续记录20 Msps的IQ数据并附带时空元数据。在2.45 GHz的 foliage、城市户外和室内办公环境下的现场实验验证了该系统可产生高保真、地理标记的IQ数据集，用于频谱表征、干扰分析和无线电环境映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频测量平台多为实验室级、成本高昂或依赖固定基础设施，限制了在快速部署、分布式或长期现场测量中的实用性。因此，需要一种便携、低成本且高保真的射频捕获系统。

Method: 集成HackRF One软件无线电、Raspberry Pi 5、GNSS接收器、稳压电池供电和高速固态存储，以SigMF格式记录连续IQ数据（最高20 Msps），并附带每段的位置和时间元数据。

Result: 密集 foliage 环境下信号接近噪底（-76至-82 dBFS）；城市环境显示30 dB动态范围的多径活动和ISM频段干扰；室内环境存在主导WiFi信道，建筑物穿透损耗约20-25 dB，干扰底噪比室外高8-10 dB。系统保持75-85 MB/s写入吞吐量，无丢样或缓冲区欠载，GNSS同步低于1秒，定位精度达米级。

Conclusion: 该便携式、低成本的SDR平台可产生高保真、地理标记的IQ数据集，适用于频谱表征、干扰分析、无线电环境映射及环境感知无线研究。

Abstract: Reliable and reproducible radio-frequency (RF) measurements in real-world environments are essential for characterizing spectrum behavior across unlicensed ISM and WiFi bands, licensed mid-band allocations, and emerging next-generation wireless deployments. Existing measurement platforms are often laboratory-grade, cost-prohibitive, or dependent on fixed infrastructure, limiting their practicality for rapid, distributed, or long-duration field campaigns. This paper presents a compact, battery-powered RF capture system integrating a HackRF One software-defined radio, Raspberry Pi 5, GNSS receiver, regulated battery supply, and high-speed solid-state storage. The platform records continuous IQ data at up to 20 Msps in SigMF format with per-segment location and timing metadata for reproducible spectrum analysis. Field experiments at 2.45 GHz in dense foliage, urban outdoor, and indoor office environments reveal distinct propagation signatures. Foliage measurements remain near the noise floor at -76 to -82 dBFS with limited spectral structure, consistent with strong canopy attenuation. Urban measurements show multipath activity across a 30 dB dynamic range, overlapping WiFi channels, and frequent ISM-band interference. Indoor measurements show dominant WiFi channels, an estimated 20 to 25 dB building entry loss relative to outdoor conditions, and an 8 to 10 dB higher interference floor caused by structural reflections. The system sustained 75 to 85 MB/s write throughput with no dropped samples or buffer underruns, while GNSS synchronization remained below one second with meter-level positioning. These results show that a portable, cost-effective SDR platform can produce high-fidelity, geotagged IQ datasets for spectrum characterization, interference analysis, radio environment mapping, and environment-aware wireless research.

</details>


### [173] [Physically-Aware Preemptive Virtual Channels for Deadlock-Free AXI Networks-on-Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01430)
*Lorenzo Leone,Luca Colagrande,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对AXI4多核SoC中读写流量协议级依赖导致的死锁问题，本文评估了四种死锁避免方案，并提出Preemptive VCs架构，相比多平面设计可节省76%链路资源，仅增加3%路由器面积开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多核SoC规模扩展，NoC需维持高内存带宽并保证无死锁。AXI4系统中读写流量的协议级依赖会在网络端点形成循环等待，传统解耦方式存在链路资源浪费（多平面）或控制复杂度过高（VC路由器）的权衡问题。

Method: 评估了四种死锁避免的AXI4流量分离方案：多平面基线和三种轻量级VC设计。其中提出Preemptive VCs，一种物理感知架构，通过优化物理层与VC的结合实现轻量化。

Result: Preemptive VCs相比多平面设计可节省高达76%的链路资源，频率相当，路由器面积开销仅增加3%。

Conclusion: 对于现代宽链路NoC，轻量级VC方案（特别是Preemptive VCs）能够在保证频率和面积效率的同时，显著降低链路资源消耗，是比多平面方案更优的AXI4流量分离设计选择。

Abstract: As many-core Systems-on-Chip (SoCs) continue to scale, Networks-on-Chip (NoCs) must sustain increasingly high memory bandwidth while preserving deadlock freedom. In AXI4 systems, protocol-level dependencies between read and write traffic can create circular waits at the network endpoints, even when the routing algorithm itself is deadlock-free. Decoupling these traffic classes avoids such dependencies, but exposes a key implementation trade-off: multiplane NoCs duplicate wide physical links and increase routing pressure, whereas conventional Virtual Channel (VC) routers add substantial control complexity, area, and timing overhead. This work revisits this trade-off for modern wide-link NoCs. We evaluate four deadlock-free AXI4 traffic-class separation schemes: a multiplane baseline and three lightweight VC-based designs. Among these designs, we propose Preemptive VCs, a physically-aware architecture that can save up to 76% of link resources with comparable frequency and only 3% router area overhead relative to the multiplane design.

</details>


### [174] [MxGLUT: A Reconfigurable LUT-Centric Broadcast Dataflow Accelerator for Mixed-Precision GEMM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01607)
*Weiyu Zhou,Chen Ding,Mingyuan Liu,Liangyu Gan,Yukun Feng,Hao Jia,Haoming Chu,Lirong Zheng,Ning Ma,Yuxiang Huan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于查找表（LUT）的可重构加速器MxGLUT，通过统一LUT计算机制和可重构广播数据流，高效支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8两种混合精度模式的LLM推理，在28nm工艺下实现了显著的面积和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LUT的加速器主要针对FP8-INT4计算，仍需独立的浮点数据路径处理注意力GEMM操作，导致硬件冗余和非统一的混合精度执行；同时其静态数据流无法适应LLM推理中prefill和decode阶段的差异化计算特征。

Method: 提出MxGLUT加速器，核心包括：(1) 混合精度LUT处理单元(MxLPE)，在单一LUT计算机制下统一支持FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8 GEMM，无需专用FP乘法器或额外FP数据路径；(2) 可重构LUT中心广播(RLB)数据流，在prefill阶段本地化累加部分和，在decode阶段利用权重复用。

Result: 28nm CMOS @200MHz综合结果显示：乘法器面积减少56.92%，功耗在FP8-INT4和FP8-FP8模式下分别降低77.07%和78.35%；加速器级面积效率0.492 TFLOPS/mm²，能效11.58 TFLOPS/W；相比仅FP8-INT4的FIGLUT基线，增加FP8-FP8支持仅损失2.57%面积效率和3.34%能效；Llama系列上prefill和decode阶段分别实现2.16×和1.49×延迟加速，能量降至0.44×和0.71×，困惑度增加不超过1.70%。

Conclusion: MxGLUT通过统一的LUT计算架构和可重构数据流，在消除冗余FP数据路径的同时高效支持混合精度LLM推理，在面积、功耗、延迟和能效方面均取得显著优势，为LLM推理加速提供了高效灵活的硬件方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference suffers from growing inefficiency across the prefill and decode phases, especially under weight-only quantization, where activations remain in FP8 while weights are compressed to low-bit integers. Existing LUT-based accelerators mainly target FP8-INT4 computation and still rely on separate floating-point (FP) datapaths for attention GEMM operations, leading to redundant hardware and non-unified mixed-precision execution. Moreover, their static dataflows are poorly matched to the distinct prefill and decode phases. To address these challenges, we propose MxGLUT, a reconfigurable LUT-centric broadcast (RLB) dataflow accelerator built on mixed-precision LUT-based processing elements (MxLPEs). Guided by a unified LUT-based execution framework, MxGLUT organizes both FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 GEMMs under a single LUT-based compute mechanism without dedicated FP multipliers or additional FP datapaths, and further adopts the RLB dataflow that localizes heavy partial-sum accumulation during the prefill phase and exploits weight reuse in the decode phase. Synthesized in UMC $28\,\mathrm{nm}$ CMOS at $200~\mathrm{MHz}$, MxGLUT reduces multiplier area by up to $56.92\%$ and power by up to $77.07\%$ and $78.35\%$ in FP8-INT4 and FP8-FP8 modes, respectively. At the accelerator level, MxGLUT achieves an area efficiency of $0.492~\mathrm{TFLOPS/mm^2}$ and an energy efficiency of $11.58~\mathrm{TFLOPS/W}$, while adding native FP8-FP8 support incurs only $2.57\%$ and $3.34\%$ reductions in area and energy efficiency, respectively, relative to the FP8-INT4-only FIGLUT baseline. Across the Llama family, MxGLUT achieves up to $2.16\times$ and $1.49\times$ latency speedup, and reduces normalized energy to $0.44\times$ and $0.71\times$ in prefill and decode, respectively, with at most $1.70\%$ perplexity increase.

</details>


### [175] [3DLS: A 3D Logic-Stacked Architecture for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01617)
*Jaehun Lee,In-Jun Jung,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 3DLS是一种逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯片架构，通过垂直互连分离KV缓存传输和解码侧TP集合通信，解决了传统2D/2.5D芯片中混合流量争用问题，实现了最高1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务中，将prefill-decode分离与张量并行结合时，传统2D/2.5D芯片架构中KV缓存传输和解码侧TP集合通信共享同一横向D2D互连，导致混合流量争用，增加通信延迟，延长token生成间隔，降低端到端服务性能。

Method: 提出3DLS逻辑-on-逻辑3D堆叠芯片架构，通过垂直互连路由KV缓存传输，同时在横向D2D网络上保留解码侧TP集合通信，从而将不同流量类型物理隔离。

Result: 与共享平面基线相比，3DLS实现了最高1.49倍吞吐量提升和60.2%的端到端延迟降低；与工作负载感知的优先级管理平面基线相比，仍实现了最高1.17倍吞吐量提升和31.4%的端到端延迟降低。

Conclusion: 物理隔离是未来基于芯片的PD分离LLM服务系统的重要设计原则。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving increasingly combines prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation with tensor parallelism (TP) to support large models and long contexts. In conventional 2D/2.5D chiplet architectures, layer-wise prefill-to-decode KV-cache transfer decode-side TP collectives share the same lateral die-to-die (D2D) interconnect, creating mixed-traffic contention on the decode critical path. This contention increases communication latency, prolongs token generation intervals, and degrades end-to-end serving performance. We propose 3DLS, a logic-on-logic 3D-stacked chiplet architecture that separates traffic classes by routing KV-cache transfers through vertical interconnects while preserving decode-side TP collectives on the lateral D2D fabric. 3DLS achieves up to 1.49$\times$ throughput and 60.2\% lower end-to-end (E2E) latency over the shared-fabric planar baseline, and still achieves up to 1.17$\times$ throughput and 31.4\% lower E2E latency over a workload-aware priority-managed planar baseline. These results highlight that physical isolation is an important design principle for future chiplet-based PD-disaggregated LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [176] [Approximate Attention Weighting for Sustainable FPGA-Based Vision Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01798)
*Muhammad Usman,Shujaat Khan,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无BRAM的近似注意力加权单元，用于在资源受限的FPGA上高效部署Vision Transformer推理。该设计采用16段分段线性函数近似softmax中的自然指数，完全基于分布式LUT实现，避免了BRAM和CORDIC管线带来的面积与功耗开销。在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现后，单注意力行单元仅消耗1444个LUT、77个DSP，且无BRAM使用；硬件精确仿真显示与精确softmax参考相比，ViT系列模型的Top-1准确率绝对差异在0.20%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小型FPGA上部署Vision Transformer面临挑战，因为自注意力中的softmax需要指数计算和归一化，硬件开销大。现有基于CORDIC或BRAM查找表的方法占用面积大、功耗高，亟需一种资源高效的近似方案。

Method: 采用16段分段线性函数近似自然指数，完全基于分布式LUT实现，无需BRAM。与基-2近似不同，自然指数形式保留了预训练的注意力温度，无需模型特定重校准。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq-7020上实现，单注意力行核心使用1444 LUT、77 DSP、0 BRAM。硬件精确仿真显示，ViT系列模型上Top-1准确率与精确softmax的绝对差异在0.20%以内。

Conclusion: 该设计证明了在资源受限的边缘AI平台上进行节能ViT推理的潜力，为FPGA部署Transformer提供了一种高效、低资源的近似方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers have reshaped computer vision by using self-attention to capture global context across image regions. This makes them attractive for edge visual inspection and monitoring in applications such as renewable-energy infrastructure, industrial quality control, medical imaging, and autonomous-system sensing. However, deploying ViTs on small FPGAs remains challenging because the softmax stage in self-attention requires exponential evaluation and normalization, which are costly in hardware. Existing implementations often rely on CORDIC pipelines or BRAM-based look-up tables, increasing area and power consumption. This paper presents a BRAM-free approximate attention-weighting unit for FPGA-based ViT inference. The proposed design approximates the natural exponential in softmax using a 16-segment piecewise-linear function implemented entirely with distributed LUT fabric. Unlike base-2 approximations, the natural-exponential formulation preserves the pre-trained attention temperature and avoids model-specific recalibration. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq-7020, the complete attention-row core uses 1444 LUTs, 77 DSPs, and no BRAM, while hardware-accurate emulation shows accuracy within a \(0.20\%\) absolute top-1 difference from the exact-softmax reference on ViT-family models. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed core for energy-efficient ViT inference on resource-constrained edge-AI platforms.

</details>


### [177] [Probabilistic Memory for Trustworthy Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02465)
*Likai Pei,Jiahao Zheng,Xueji Zhao,Emilie Ye,Jianbo Liu,Hanqing Tao,Ming-Yen Lee,Ruiyang Qin,Yiyu Shi,Shimeng Yu,X. Sharon Hu,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出概率存储器(p-MEM)，一种统一存储原语，可直接存储分布参数并以原生内存带宽进行高斯随机数采样，实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的吞吐量，在贝叶斯神经网络任务中相比传统方案减少指令数最多4.37倍、延迟最多562倍、能耗最多295.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率计算在可信边缘智能中至关重要，但高斯随机数生成(GRNG)与计算之间存在数量级的数据吞吐量差距，且存在指令开销问题，限制了其采用。

Method: 提出p-MEM统一存储原语，存储均值与标准差等分布参数，在原生内存带宽下直接采样，将确定性数据视为零方差特例；通过布局验证的模拟器探索器件选择、内存规格和技术节点。

Result: p-MEM可实现超过1000 GSa/s/mm²的GRNG吞吐量（含存储阵列访问）；集成到CPU/GPU系统中，在贝叶斯神经网络任务上指令数减少2.19倍/4.37倍，采样延迟降低562倍/3.45倍，能耗降低295.5倍/3.53倍。

Conclusion: p-MEM为可信概率AI提供了可扩展的硬件基础，有效弥合了随机数生成与计算之间的吞吐量差距。

Abstract: Probabilistic computation plays an important role in trustworthy edge intelligence to quantify uncertainty, enhance robustness, reconstruct data, and protect privacy, but its adoption is limited by the orders-of-magnitude data throughput gap between Gaussian random number generation (GRNG) and computation, as well as instruction overhead. This paper introduces probabilistic memory (p-MEM), a unified memory primitive that stores distribution parameters, such as mean and standard deviation, and samples directly at the native memory bandwidth, where deterministic data becomes the zero-variance special case. Using a layout-validated p-MEM simulator, we comprehensively explore device choices, memory specifications, and technology nodes, showing that p-MEM can achieve more than 1000 GSa/s/mm^2 GRNG throughput, including memory-array access. Integrated into CPU/GPU systems, p-MEM reduces instruction count by up to 2.19x/4.37x, sampling latency by 562x/3.45x, and energy by 295.5x/3.53x for Bayesian neural network workloads, providing a scalable hardware substrate for trustworthy probabilistic AI.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个模块化的神经符号框架，用于生成考虑可行性的反事实解释。该框架将神经预测模型与符号推理层分离，通过显式建模领域约束来生成既有效又可行的解释。在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能够产生更符合领域可行性要求的解释，展示了神经符号方法在可解释AI中实现透明且可行性感知的反事实解释的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测结果的输入变化，但由于缺乏显式机制来整合领域知识和干预约束，往往产生不现实或不可行的建议。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用模块化神经符号架构，将预测与推理分离为两个组件：神经预测模型（用于分类）和符号推理层（在反事实生成过程中强制执行领域特定约束）。方法具有模型无关性，可适应需要现实决策支持的领域。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上，结合多层感知机分类器与回答集编程（ASP）规则进行案例研究。结果显示符号约束能产生更符合领域可行性要求的解释，并揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法能够有效平衡反事实有效性与可行性，为生成透明、可行性感知的反事实解释提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [179] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一个用于联邦学习算法配方搜索的受约束编码代理工作流。代理可提出并实现候选训练算法，在五个FLamby医疗任务和六个LEAF任务上评估，部分任务获得提升，但存在种子敏感性和失败案例。通过同预算控制实验，将改进分解为重复的FL机制、固定表面调优效应和单次运行伪迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL研究中存在大量小而重要的算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、训练调度等），这些选择人工探索成本高且难以公平比较，因为候选变更可能同时改变训练或评估路径。

Method: 提出AFR框架，采用受约束编码代理工作流进行FL算法配方搜索。代理可提出并实现候选训练算法（服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、局部目标函数、注册模型变体），任务配置文件固定突变表面、计算预算、通信合约和最终模型评估。每次实验记录候选得分、运行时间、编辑文件、工件和失败状态。

Result: 在5个FLamby医疗交叉-silo任务和6个LEAF任务（5个固定+1个合成）上评估，五种子重复实验支持4个FLamby任务和5/6个LEAF任务获得提升，同时暴露了种子敏感性和搜索选择的失败案例。同预算控制实验表明，部分收益来自FL配方变更，其他改进可由固定表面标量控制恢复，或在重复/保留评估下失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果本身是贡献：展示了如何将代理生成的候选方案区分为重复的FL机制、固定表面调优效应和选择的单次运行伪迹。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [180] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个完全原创的小型语言模型架构，包含五种新颖组件：螺旋旋转位置编码、门控跨层注意力、自适应令牌合并、双流前馈网络和WiolaRMSNorm，在四种规模上发布并兼容HuggingFace生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型架构（如GPT、LLaMA、Mistral等）在结构上高度相似，缺乏从第一性原理出发的原创设计探索。作者旨在构建一个完全不继承任何现有模型家族结构血统的全新SLM架构。

Method: 提出五种独立新颖组件：(i) SRPE，在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置信息，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；(ii) GCLA，每个解码层可软性交叉注意访问前两层的压缩摘要；(iii) ATM，在中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻令牌以降低注意力复杂度；(iv) DSFF，用两个并行流替代传统MLP，通过学习的逐维门控融合；(v) WiolaRMSNorm，引入逐维学习偏移向量防止表示坍塌。提供完整数学推导、框图、复杂度分析及与GPT-2/LLaMA-2/Mistral的系统比较。

Result: Wiola 发布了四种规模（120M、360M、700M、1.5B参数），完全兼容HuggingFace Transformers生态系统，所有22个架构单元测试通过。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理设计不继承现有模型血统的全新架构是可行的，五种创新组件共同构成了一个有竞争力的SLM方案。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [181] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent4cs，一种多智能体框架，通过自底向上方式、结合总结、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，对大型代码库进行摘要生成，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖率上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方法通常依赖单一语言模型或编码助手（如Claude Code），将源码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码仓库中丰富的相互依赖关系和层次结构信息，导致对大型、复杂代码库的理解仍然困难重重。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上（bottom-up）方式进行代码库摘要：1）总结智能体（Summarization Agent）负责生成稳健的摘要；2）关键词提取智能体（Keyword-Extraction Agent）主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；3）质量保证智能体（Quality-Assurance Agent）迭代优化输出以确保可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级上平均提升8%的语义一致性；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率相比基线方法提升高达38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs通过多智能体协作和层次化处理范式，有效改善了大型代码库的摘要质量，超越了基于结构化提示词和代码片段的传统基线方法。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [182] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将有操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流，利用冲突感知通信和写前重新考虑机制，在零售和航空任务中提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体从对话接口向操作执行角色转变（退款、取消、改单等），需要在保持常规服务快速低摩擦的同时，防止因客户指令、政策约束、企业记录和后端写入交互导致的运营错误。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：(1) 轻量级路由器，将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合会话路由到升级工作流；(2) 升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写触发重新考虑机制，将审查和安全措施集中在有后果的后端写入之前，而非对所有会话统一施加额外控制。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升可靠性；路由证据表明强控制指向冲突请求而非常规请求；对话和工具使用分析显示提升来自证据收集、写入分离和写前重新考虑，而非无差别扩展交互或工具链；案例证据显示升级工作流保留备用计划、绑定记录到正确动作、排序写入、分解多实体请求。航空任务验证了相同逻辑的适用性。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能有效区分常规会话与操作耦合会话，将审查资源集中于真正需要关注的冲突场景，在保持常规服务效率的同时提升复杂操作场景的可靠性，适用于零售和航空等不同领域。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [183] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的方法，通过对比权重引导增强LLM的 divergent thinking（发散思维），在多项创造力测试中提升性能并减少模式崩溃，且无需重新训练或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在开放性问题中倾向于生成相似响应，即"人工蜂群思维效应"(artificial hivemind effect)，限制了其创造性输出的多样性。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro，一种基于对比权重引导(contrastive weight steering)的无数据方法，通过调整模型权重空间来增强发散思维，无需行为数据、重新训练或梯度微调。

Result: 在DAT任务上提升高达14个百分位；在AUT和Task Task的大规模人工评估(N=720)中，在原创性、惊喜度和创造力方面显著提升；在所有三项任务中均减少了模式崩溃。激活引导(activation steering)在DAT上表现相当但无法迁移到AUT和Task Task，而权重空间引导(weight-space steering)具有更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro有效提升LLM发散思维并减少模式崩溃，是一种简单直接的创意增强方法，无需额外数据或复杂训练流程。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [184] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）在医学视觉问答任务上性能与自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）持平或更优，解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，且具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，更适合临床报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学基础模型几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在文本生成领域已展现竞争力，因此探索将其引入医学领域以克服自回归模型的局限。

Method: 将混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B与同尺寸自回归模型Gemma-4-26B在医学视觉问答数据集上进行相同LoRA微调后对比，使用对冗长鲁棒的LLM裁判评分。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医学视觉问答任务上匹配或超越自回归模型，微调模型（3.8B活跃参数）与前沿视觉语言模型竞争力相当，解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医学文本生成中不仅性能持平或超越自回归模型，其固有的任意顺序填充能力更符合临床报告的碎片化、不一致性特点，具有独特优势。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在目标企业SaaS环境中直接使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练小语言模型，以解决LLM在嵌套API调用中因目标不匹配导致的静默失败问题。在五个模拟Jira/Confluence API的合成环境中，经GRPO训练的4B模型在四个非退化场景上将平均奖励从0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，最大提升为Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）。但手动设计可验证奖励在规模上受限，且一个场景存在奖励饱和问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面向企业SaaS API（如Jira、Confluence）的嵌套调用中存在目标不匹配问题——模型训练目标是预测下一个token而非在具体API中正确行动，导致静默失败（漏填必填字段、幻觉工具、单次读取后提前停止）。

Method: 构建五个模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API的合成环境（保真至schema级别），在该环境中应用GRPO算法；奖励完全从工具调用轨迹计算，不使用真实API、学习型评判器或人工标注。训练Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B模型，使用与GRPO训练相同的检查器进行评测。

Result: 在四个非退化场景中，RL训练的策略将平均奖励从4B基线范围0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00；最大增益为Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）；票证流转（ticket-transition）场景奖励已饱和，4B基线即达最大值。

Conclusion: 这项工作是为企业级API场景训练面向结果优化的小模型迈出的初步步骤；两个限制：手动设计可验证奖励难以扩展到少量端点之外，以及部分场景奖励函数已饱和。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap.
